# <b><c1/></b>: RINEX clock files, versions { "2" } and { "3.00" } to { "3.04" } (including the { "3.04" } nine-character name field). The header is read only for its <c2/> and is skipped up to <c3/>. Of the data records, only the <c4/> (satellite) and <c5/> (receiver or station) records of one clock, selected by name, are kept. Their first value field, the clock bias in seconds, is the phase. Continuation lines and the <c6/>, <c7/> and <c8/> records are skipped. # How a run flows. A scenario TOML file (a kind, a seed and its parameters) goes into the engine, kshana { $version }, through the api::run_toml dispatch over { "87" } scenario kinds, deterministic from scenario, seed and engine version. The engine writes result.json, chart.svg, report.html and report.json, a table.csv for the kinds that define one, and on request SP3, CCSDS OMM and OEM, CZML, KML, GeoJSON, STK and SigMF exports; a suite writes study.json and study.html. Those files feed Kshana Studio in the browser, an AI assistant through the kshana-mcp server, and continuous integration.
# attr:alt
seg-76e3ec458441 = Como uma execução flui. Um arquivo TOML de cenário (um tipo de cenário, uma semente e seus parâmetros) entra no motor, kshana { $version }, pelo despacho api::run_toml sobre { "87" } tipos de cenário, determinístico a partir do cenário, da semente e da versão do motor. O motor escreve result.json, chart.svg, report.html e report.json, um table.csv para os tipos de cenário que definem um e, sob pedido, exportações SP3, CCSDS OMM e OEM, CZML, KML, GeoJSON, STK e SigMF; uma suíte escreve study.json e study.html. Esses arquivos alimentam o Kshana Studio no navegador, um assistente de IA por meio do servidor kshana-mcp e a integração contínua.

Continuous integration (CI) makes it impossible to call a capability VALIDATED without an independent external oracle.
# text
seg-707e2cf795f6 = <b>{ "176" } de { "388" }</b> capacidades validadas contra referências independentes externas; { "208" } honestamente rotuladas como modeladas. Cada linha da matriz de verificação nomeia uma capacidade, a referência independente contra a qual ela é verificada, o teste que executa a verificação e o rótulo que resulta. A integração contínua (CI) torna impossível chamar uma capacidade de VALIDATED sem uma referência independente externa.

# How a capability earns its label. Each row of the verification matrix names a capability, the oracle it is checked against, the test that runs the check in continuous integration, and the label that follows. A row may be VALIDATED only with an independent external oracle; otherwise it is MODELLED, or PARTNER when a hardware partner owns it. Example: SGP4/SDP4, Cowell 6-DOF + perturbations, batch/sequential OD, oracle AIAA 2006-6753 SGP4 verification vectors, test tests/sgp4_verification.rs, label VALIDATED. Live counts: { "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER of { "388" } rows. In all, { "176" } capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } more are honestly labelled MODELLED and { "4" } are PARTNER-owned.
# attr:alt
seg-2f81cf391eae = Como uma capacidade conquista seu rótulo. Cada linha da matriz de verificação nomeia uma capacidade, a referência independente contra a qual ela é verificada, o teste que executa a verificação na integração contínua e o rótulo que resulta. Uma linha só pode ser VALIDATED com uma referência independente externa; caso contrário é MODELLED, ou PARTNER quando um parceiro de hardware é o responsável. Exemplo: SGP4/SDP4, Cowell 6-DOF + perturbações, OD em lote/sequencial, referência independente: vetores de verificação do SGP4 AIAA 2006-6753, teste tests/sgp4_verification.rs, rótulo VALIDATED. Contagens ao vivo: { "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER de { "388" } linhas. No total, { "176" } capacidades validadas contra referências independentes externas; { "208" } outras são honestamente rotuladas MODELLED e { "4" } têm como responsável um PARTNER.

# Kshana's architecture: one open engine at the centre, kshana { $version } under the AGPL-3.0, with api::run_toml, a typed dispatch over every kind, and the verification ledger of { "388" } capabilities ({ "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER). Around it, every surface runs the same engine: Command line (cargo install kshana); Rust library (cargo add kshana); Python (pip install kshana); WebAssembly + Kshana Studio (npm install kshana); MCP server (cargo install kshana-mcp); Docker image (ghcr.io/ashfordeou/kshana-mcp); JetBrains plugin (Marketplace: "Kshana"). Below it, Kshana Pro, a proprietary overlay that depends on the open engine as a library and never forks it, and adds no physical model.
# attr:alt
seg-9d69cf43cd40 = Arquitetura do Kshana: um motor aberto no centro, kshana { $version } sob a AGPL-3.0, com api::run_toml, um despacho tipado sobre todos os tipos de cenário, e o registro de verificação de { "388" } capacidades ({ "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER). Ao redor, todas as superfícies executam o mesmo motor: linha de comando (cargo install kshana); biblioteca Rust (cargo add kshana); Python (pip install kshana); WebAssembly + Kshana Studio (npm install kshana); servidor MCP (cargo install kshana-mcp); imagem Docker (ghcr.io/ashfordeou/kshana-mcp); plugin do JetBrains (Marketplace: "Kshana"). Abaixo, o Kshana Pro, uma camada proprietária que depende do motor aberto como biblioteca, nunca o bifurca e não acrescenta nenhum modelo físico.

# Kshana's architecture in layers. Five front doors (command line, Python, JavaScript and WebAssembly, the MCP server and the JetBrains plugin) reach one api::run_toml, a typed dispatch over { "87" } kinds. Beneath it sit eight domain layers: time and frames, clocks and timing, inertial and fusion, GNSS and integrity, astrodynamics, LEO PNT, Moon, Mars and deep space, and missions and studies, on a shared core, all cross-referenced by the verification module, the machine-checked matrix of { "388" } capabilities that is the single source of truth for every label.
# attr:alt
seg-9bc9a971bdb4 = Arquitetura do Kshana em camadas. # <b>{ "176" } of { "388" }</b> capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } honestly labelled Modelled, { "4" } partner-owned. Each capability carries one label in a machine-checked ledger: VALIDATED (an independent external oracle agrees: real data, an independent implementation or published reference vectors), MODELLED (internally consistent, and said out loud) or PARTNER (a hardware partner owns it). # Three independent tools, pre-registered (0123cee1) before any fixture or oracle output existed, tolerances fixed then. ({ "1" }) ARAIM: Stanford MAAST for ARAIM { "2" } (commit ab70e2a3, BSD-3) mhss_raim_baseline_v5.m, run as a separate program under GNU Octave { "8.4" } with a driver-side unique() compatibility shim (disclosed, no algorithm change), at the ADD TOL_PL of { "0.05" } m: the Rust reproduces the ADD reference implementation on { "270" } real cases. Disclosed: the Kshana ADD path was written after the pre-registration, following the ADD as MAAST implements it; an independent hold-out by the reviewer ({ "270" } new user geometries and a second integrity support message, MAAST re-run against the frozen code) passed at the same bar. ({ "2" }) Snapshot RAIM: RTKLIB v2.4.2-p13 (BSD-2) estpos/valsol/raim_fde in a separate C harness for detection and exclusion; the slope PL from RTKLIB matinv/xyz2enu plus SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2, where both sides evaluate the same closed form (Brown; Parkinson and Axelrad), so the PL check covers the implementation, not the formula. ({ "3" }) SBAS: Stanford MAAST (commit 7d32b049) usr_vhpl, unmodified, on MAAST own recorded WAAS broadcasts: L1 levels within { "1e-4" } m after the K rescaling and an equal protected set. The same comparisons produced the findings stated in the capability: the uniform-sigma ARAIM functions are not ADD-conformant, and the L5 set equality fails on { "308" } pairs with no GEO in view (the levels on the { "3267" } pairs both tools protect agree to { "1e-13" } m). The DO-229E/DO-316 K-factors are transcribed constants and the real geometry is an input; neither is counted as an oracle. { "0.30" } revision: snapshot_raim protection levels moved from the radial to the geodetic local level (up to { "0.31" } m HPL and { "0.14" } m VPL at { "16" } deg N)

seg-a83c68500982 = Três ferramentas independentes, com fixação antecipada (0123cee1) antes de existir qualquer fixture ou saída de referência independente, com tolerâncias fixadas então. ({ "1" }) ARAIM: Stanford MAAST para ARAIM { "2" } (commit ab70e2a3, BSD-3) mhss_raim_baseline_v5.m, executado como programa separado sob o GNU Octave { "8.4" } com uma camada de compatibilidade unique() do lado do driver (divulgada, sem alteração de algoritmo), com o TOL_PL do ADD de { "0.05" } m: o Rust reproduz a implementação de referência do ADD em { "270" } casos reais. Divulgado: o caminho ADD do Kshana foi escrito após o pré-registro, seguindo o ADD como o MAAST o implementa; um conjunto reservado independente feito pelo revisor ({ "270" } novas geometrias de usuário e uma segunda mensagem de suporte à integridade, com o MAAST executado de novo contra o código congelado) passou no mesmo limiar. ({ "2" }) RAIM instantâneo: RTKLIB v2.4.2-p13 (BSD-2) estpos/valsol/raim_fde em um harness separado em C para detecção e exclusão; o PL de inclinação a partir de matinv/xyz2enu do RTKLIB mais chi2/ncx2 do SciPy { "1.18.1" }, em que ambos os lados avaliam a mesma forma fechada (Brown; Parkinson e Axelrad), de modo que a verificação do PL cobre a implementação, não a fórmula. ({ "3" }) SBAS: Stanford MAAST (commit 7d32b049) usr_vhpl, sem modificação, nas próprias transmissões WAAS gravadas do MAAST: níveis L1 a { "1e-4" } m após o reescalonamento de K e um conjunto protegido igual. As mesmas comparações produziram os achados declarados na capacidade: as funções de ARAIM de sigma uniforme não são aderentes à norma ADD, e a igualdade de conjuntos do L5 falha em { "308" } pares sem GEO em visada (os níveis nos { "3267" } pares que ambas as ferramentas protegem concordam a { "1e-13" } m). Os fatores K de DO-229E/DO-316 são constantes transcritas e a geometria real é uma entrada; nenhum dos dois é contado como referência independente. Revisão { "0.30" }: os níveis de proteção de snapshot_raim passaram do nível local radial para o geodésico (até { "0.31" } m de HPL e { "0.14" } m de VPL a { "16" } graus N)

WHY MODELLED, and it is the transmit pattern: the gain at angle is a uniformly illuminated circular aperture, the Airy pattern, while a real GPS L1 antenna is a twelve-element helical array with a shaped main lobe peaked off-boresight to even out power across the Earth disc and sidelobes that are not Airy. Published measured patterns exist and are not vendored here, so orderings and orders of magnitude carry, while the dB of any single satellite does not. A FIRST IMPLEMENTATION OF THIS ROW WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the Airy expression is valid only in the forward hemisphere, its argument goes as sin(theta), and at theta near { "180" } deg it wraps around and returns FULL BORESIGHT GAIN behind the aperture - so the two strongest links in the first report were satellites pointing their antennas away from the Moon, and they were the only two that cleared the threshold. Behind the aperture the model now applies a flat back-lobe floor, stated as a bound rather than a fabricated pattern shape. Deliberately absent, each making the budget OPTIMISTIC: no ionospheric or tropospheric loss on the limb-grazing rays, no polarisation, pointing or implementation loss, and a spherical Earth with no refractive extension. The Moon position is an INPUT, not an ephemeris lookup, because the quantity under test is the link and the beam geometry. # Two internal oracles, no external dataset. ({ "1" }) Closed-form round trip: the minimum detectable bias is produced by inverting the non-central chi-square tail on the non-centrality (raim::pbias) and is verified by feeding the resulting non-centrality back through raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf at the monitor's own threshold, which must return P_md. ({ "2" }) Injection vs analysis: a bias is written into the RF estimate of one axis and cross_raim::run_cross_raim is re-run, so the statistic compared against the analytic non-centrality is the one the monitor computed, not a re-derivation. A seeded Monte-Carlo of the statistic itself is carried in the tests as a third check; it is deliberately NOT in the report, because a sampled estimate would be slower and not reproducible bit-for-bit. ExternalDataset is declined: the quantity is the detection power of THIS monitor at THIS scenario's sigma allocation, and the sigma magnitudes the MDB is scaled by are Modelled representative inputs, not measurements. ReferenceImpl was also considered and declined — the Monte-Carlo samples the same statistic the analysis describes, so it catches transcription and coefficient errors but is not an independent implementation of the monitor. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (pre-registered 0135a9e4), blocked (stays MODELLED): SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2/brentq reproduce the report threshold (within { "5.4e-12" }), per-axis MDB, power curve, crossing multiple and ramp time-to-detect (within { "5.3e-10" }) on five configurations; the injected-fault statistics have no independent oracle and the sigma magnitudes are Modelled inputs, so the scoping awaits an owner decision

({ "2" }) Injeção versus análise: um viés é escrito na estimativa de RF de um eixo e cross_raim::run_cross_raim é executado de novo, de modo que a estatística comparada com a não centralidade analítica é a que o monitor calculou, e não uma rederivação. Um Monte Carlo com semente da própria estatística é mantido nos testes como terceira verificação; ele NÃO está no relatório de propósito, porque uma estimativa amostrada seria mais lenta e não reproduzível bit a bit. ExternalDataset é recusado: a grandeza é o poder de detecção DESTE monitor na alocação de sigma DESTE cenário, e as magnitudes de sigma pelas quais o MDB é escalado são entradas representativas modeladas, não medições. # An independent in-repo route. Every Jacobian row is re-derived by central finite difference of lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s evaluated from the state's own Earth-fixed and Moon-body-fixed coordinates rotated forward through the frame chain — a path that computes no derivative and shares no expression with the analytic partials — and the information matrices the two routes build agree to < { "1e-6" } of the largest diagonal. The linear-algebra kernel this composes (information_matrix, crlb, sym_eig) is separately externally anchored against numpy.linalg.eigh / numpy.linalg.inv in tests/fim_observability_reference.rs, and the delay observable carries lunar_vlbi's own delta-DOR far-field oracle; the row does not borrow either status. Closed-form identities checked here and labelled as such: σ² and 1/N scaling, and √(p/trace(M)) as an AM-HM lower bound the computed sigma cannot beat, which coincides with the equipartition value only on an isotropic geometry — which is why the measured ratio is exactly the anisotropy the assumption discarded. { "0.30" } external comparison (Library: NAIF SPICE CSPICE N0067 via spiceypy { "8.2.0" }, MIT; DE440, ITRF93 earth_latest_high_prec, MOON_PA), pre-registered ({ "50863551" }) together with an engine-side model-error sensitivity run: every Jacobian row is rebuilt by central differences of converged Newtonian light-time differences from SPICE geometry (the light-time equation is a short script over SPICE positions; SPICE carries the geometry), with the schedule rebuilt from SPICE elevations. The schedule, rank, defect, information spectrum, condition number, station sigmas, headline, trace bound, equipartition ratios, free-network null space, line-of-sight sweep and beacon declination agree within the pre-registered { "1" } % / { "0.1" } deg, which sits above the engine known analytic-Moon error ({ "3.2e-3" } on sigmas, measured before the run). NumPy { "2.3.5" } redoes the linear algebra on the committed engine Jacobian to { "2e-13" } (P2). Validated as a Cramér-Rao bound for the stated reduced parameter set on the stated illustrative inputs: the Moon-centre ephemeris, clocks, troposphere and Earth-orientation parameters are held fixed in both legs, so it is not a real-campaign accuracy

Cada linha do Jacobiano é rederivada por diferença finita central de lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s avaliada a partir das próprias coordenadas fixas à Terra e fixas ao corpo lunar do estado, rotacionadas adiante pela cadeia de referenciais — um caminho que não calcula nenhuma derivada e não compartilha nenhuma expressão com as derivadas parciais analíticas — e as matrizes de informação que as duas rotas constroem concordam a menos de { "1e-6" } da maior diagonal. O núcleo de álgebra linear que isso compõe (information_matrix, crlb, sym_eig) tem âncora externa separada em relação a numpy.linalg.eigh / numpy.linalg.inv em tests/fim_observability_reference.rs, e a observável de atraso carrega a própria referência independente de campo distante do delta-DOR de lunar_vlbi; a linha não toma emprestado nenhum dos dois estados. Identidades em forma fechada verificadas aqui e rotuladas como tais: escala de σ² e 1/N, e √(p/trace(M)) como um limite inferior AM-HM que o sigma calculado não pode superar, o qual coincide com o valor de equipartição apenas em uma geometria isotrópica — razão pela qual a razão medida é exatamente a anisotropia que a hipótese descartou. { "0.30" } comparação externa (Biblioteca: NAIF SPICE CSPICE N0067 via spiceypy { "8.2.0" }, MIT; DE440, ITRF93 earth_latest_high_prec, MOON_PA), pré-registrada ({ "50863551" }) junto com uma execução de sensibilidade a erro de modelo do lado do motor: cada linha do Jacobiano é reconstruída por diferenças centrais de diferenças de tempo de luz newtonianas convergidas a partir da geometria SPICE (a equação do tempo de luz é um script curto sobre posições SPICE; o SPICE traz a geometria), com o cronograma reconstruído a partir de elevações SPICE. O cronograma, o posto, o defeito, o espectro de informação, o número de condição, os sigmas de estação, o valor principal, o limite de traço, as razões de equipartição, o espaço nulo da rede livre, a varredura da linha de visada e a declinação do farol concordam dentro dos { "1" } % / { "0.1" } graus pré-registrados, o que fica acima do erro analítico conhecido da Lua do motor ({ "3.2e-3" } nos sigmas, medido antes da execução). O NumPy { "2.3.5" } refaz a álgebra linear sobre o Jacobiano confirmado do motor até { "2e-13" } (P2). Validado como limite de Cramér-Rao para o conjunto reduzido de parâmetros declarado nas entradas ilustrativas declaradas: a efeméride do centro da Lua, os relógios, a troposfera e os parâmetros de orientação da Terra são mantidos fixos nas duas etapas, de modo que não é uma exatidão de campanha real

# Closed-form identities and an independent in-repo route, with no external oracle claimed. The Helmert design — the only new derivative in the module — is re-derived by central finite difference of lunar_frame_realise::apply_helmert, a path sharing no expression with the analytic form. The accumulation it composes is lunar-vlbi-fim's, whose Jacobian is finite-differenced against lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s there, and whose linear-algebra kernel is separately externally anchored against numpy in tests/fim_observability_reference.rs — this row borrows neither status. The physics oracle is structural rather than numerical: a beacon delay partial is the near-field DIFFERENCE of two near-parallel unit vectors, so the worst-determined translation direction MUST be the body-fixed direction to Earth, and the test asserts the computed answer against the separately measured direction. MEASURED: campaign-derived translation sigma { "6.3975" } m against the injected-transform scenario's { "0.3125" } m recovery error ({ "20.5" }× tighter), while rotation and scale run the OTHER way at { "2.32" }× and { "13.5" }× looser — the injected path assumes one isotropic sigma and so spreads its error uniformly across seven parameters, which the delay observable does not. # Internal consistency: the brute-force elevation scan, the hand-computed and orbit::dop reductions, and the closed-form Walker and geosynchronous geometry. # WHAT IS EXTERNALLY CHECKED, stated narrowly: our Prometheus text exposition for an 84-line synthetic stream (label values with quotes, backslashes, line feeds and non-ASCII text, { "64" } reason series plus an 'other' fold, gate and position gauges) is accepted by prometheus_client { "0.26.0" }'s text parser, and the metric families, types, help text, label sets and values it reads equal the registry's state exactly (values by f64 equality). Our OTLP/HTTP JSON for the same registry parses with protobuf { "7.36.2" } json_format.Parse(ignore_unknown_fields=False) into opentelemetry-proto { "1.45.1" }'s ExportMetricsServiceRequest, and every metric name, description, unit, kind, monotonicity, aggregation temporality, attribute, value, start time and time equals the registry's state exactly. Both parsers were shown to reject an unknown field, a mistyped value and a malformed line (scripts/gen_telemetry_formats_ref.py). Schema { "1.3" } ({ "0.36" }), pre-registered (fb226057) before any tool read it: for a 64-line synthetic schema-1.3 stream (geometry statuses, DOPs, labels and signal loss present, null, absent and unknown; deduction classes; unparsable lines) the same two parsers read the geometry series (Prometheus kshana_trust_geometry*, OTLP kshana.geometry.*) and the class label on kshana_trust_reason_active exactly as the registry implies ({ "24" } families and { "54" } samples; { "12" } metrics and { "31" } points), promtool { "3.5.0" } check metrics exits { "0" } with no lint problem, and the OpenTelemetry Collector contrib { "0.135.0" } accepts the OTLP/HTTP JSON (status { "200" }, nothing rejected) and its file exporter records the request unchanged (xval/telemetry-1.3, data-gated: the release tarballs are fetched with pinned SHA-256 and their outputs committed). WHAT IS NOT: the HTTP endpoint, delivery, timeouts and reconnection are not checked by an oracle, nor the Collector's prometheus receiver; the registry is read through accessors, so a fault in the registry state itself would not be caught; the OTLP 'committed output is current' check runs only with the otlp feature; the metric names and their usefulness to an operations centre are not validated; CEF and LEEF are not covered (see the next row).

Amendments ({ "1" }) to ({ "3" }) touched no comparison rule and no expected value: ({ "1" }) a corpus-size assertion was corrected from { "5" } to { "4" } band changes, a miscount, since the stream has { "4" }; ({ "2" }) the Logstash runner's pipeline delimiter wiring was fixed after a first attempt that produced no events; ({ "3" }) the trust score and epoch offset were moved from the CEF integer fields cn1 and cn2 to the floating-point fields cfp1 and cfp2, a wire-format change made in this release, after which the corpus was regenerated and both oracles were re-run with the bar unchanged and met it again. Amendment ({ "4" }), { "0.36" }, extended the corpus and the expected key set, registered in d6331429 before either parser ran on the new lines: schema { "1.3" } inputs add the satellite geometry in the custom fields cs6 (label geometry: status, then ';' and the deduction classes), cfp3 (geometryHdop) and cfp4 (geometryPdop); { "13" } geometry cases (the corpus to { "170" } lines) and the three new keys; both met the bar, otherwise unchanged, at the first comparison (pycef { "166" } of { "170" } lines parsed, the { "4" } tier { "2" } header cases as before; Logstash { "170" } of { "170" }). Amendment ({ "5" }), { "0.36" }, appended { "6" } geometry-label cases (the corpus to { "176" } lines), registered in 9a8e4fa9 before either parser ran on them: from inputs that carry the geometry label, cs6 gains a third field (';' and the label) and cn1 (geometryUsedSatellites) the satellites used; the Logstash codec's mapping table was read first (no flexString fields, hence cs6 rather than flexString1); both met the bar at the first comparison (pycef { "172" } of { "176" }, Logstash { "176" } of { "176" }), and every earlier reference entry was reproduced unchanged. seg-ce6d0ecab448 = O servidor é um <b>crate independente, excluído do workspace</b>, com seu próprio <c1/>, de modo que o SDK oficial de MCP em Rust (kit de desenvolvimento de software) <c2/> e seu runtime assíncrono nunca entram no grafo de dependências do crate <c3/> publicado: quem usa a biblioteca ou o Python não compila um servidor que não executa. (Ele foi separado primeiro porque <c4/> é <b>edition { "2024" }</b>, exigindo rustc ≥ { "1.85" }, enquanto o crate principal então prometia { "1.75" }. A biblioteca agora declara { "1.85" }; este servidor declara { "1.88" }, o piso do <c5/> { "2" }.x corrigido quanto à segurança.) A integração contínua (CI) controla este crate por conta própria — testes e <c6/> contra o seu lockfile. A árvore de licenças do rmcp está limpa (Apache-2.0/MIT/BSD), de modo que, ao contrário do crate de validação cruzada <c7/>, que ele de resto espelha, a separação não tem a ver com licenciamento.

# checked against its own closed-form / analytic identity — catches transcription and coefficient errors, but is not an external oracle — Closed-form identities and internal consistency; NO external oracle is claimed. The limb half angle is checked against asin(R_earth/r_orbit) and the geometry against it; the free-space loss, the DOP kernel and the SGP4 propagation this composes are each externally validated in their own rows and this row does not borrow their status. WHY MODELLED, and it is the transmit pattern: the gain at angle is a uniformly illuminated circular aperture, the Airy pattern, while a real GPS L1 antenna is a twelve-element helical array with a shaped main lobe peaked off-boresight to even out power across the Earth disc and sidelobes that are not Airy. Published measured patterns exist and are not vendored here, so orderings and orders of magnitude carry, while the dB of any single satellite does not. A FIRST IMPLEMENTATION OF THIS ROW WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the Airy expression is valid only in the forward hemisphere, its argument goes as sin(theta), and at theta near { "180" } deg it wraps around and returns FULL BORESIGHT GAIN behind the aperture - so the two strongest links in the first report were satellites pointing their antennas away from the Moon, and they were the only two that cleared the threshold. Behind the aperture the model now applies a flat back-lobe floor, stated as a bound rather than a fabricated pattern shape. Deliberately absent, each making the budget OPTIMISTIC: no ionospheric or tropospheric loss on the limb-grazing rays, no polarisation, pointing or implementation loss, and a spherical Earth with no refractive extension. The Moon position is an INPUT, not an ephemeris lookup, because the quantity under test is the link and the beam geometry. Upgrading this row to Validated needs a measured transmit pattern and LuGRE normal points to check against; neither is in the repository and neither is invented here. { "0.30" } round { "2" }, a finding (stays MODELLED): a measured-input link path (real SP3 positions, per-block transmit power, tabulated transmit and receive patterns, acquisition and tracking hysteresis) was checked against Montenbruck et al. { "2023" } Table { "3" } for GENESIS at { "6000" } km (pre-registered 5b414f0a): zenith-antenna means agree on { "5" } of { "6" } within one dB of the paper sensitivity; nadir-antenna means are { "1.0" } to { "2.4" } satellites higher than printed, plausibly because the paper used azimuth-dependent GPS side-lobe patterns that are not published. seg-ed40da9bdfae = verificado contra a sua própria identidade analítica / de forma fechada — detecta erros de transcrição e de coeficiente, mas não é uma referência independente externa — Identidades de forma fechada e consistência interna; NENHUMA referência independente externa é alegada. POR QUE MODELLED, e é o padrão de transmissão: o ganho em função do ângulo é o de uma abertura circular uniformemente iluminada, o padrão de Airy, enquanto uma antena GPS L1 real é um arranjo helicoidal de doze elementos com um lóbulo principal moldado, com pico fora do eixo para uniformizar a potência sobre o disco da Terra, e com lóbulos laterais que não são de Airy. Existem padrões medidos publicados e eles não estão incluídos aqui, de modo que ordenações e ordens de grandeza se sustentam, mas o dB de qualquer satélite isolado não. UMA PRIMEIRA IMPLEMENTAÇÃO DESTA LINHA ESTAVA ERRADA DE UM MODO QUE VALE REGISTRAR: a expressão de Airy só é válida no hemisfério frontal, o seu argumento vai com sin(theta), e perto de theta = { "180" } graus ela dá a volta e devolve O GANHO COMPLETO NO EIXO atrás da abertura - de modo que os dois enlaces mais fortes do primeiro relatório eram satélites apontando suas antenas para longe da Lua, e eram os dois únicos que superavam o limiar. Atrás da abertura o modelo agora aplica um piso plano de lóbulo traseiro, declarado como um limite e não como uma forma de padrão fabricada. Deliberadamente ausentes, cada um tornando o orçamento OTIMISTA: nenhuma perda ionosférica ou troposférica nos raios que tangenciam o limbo, nenhuma perda de polarização, apontamento ou implementação, e uma Terra esférica sem extensão refrativa. # checked against its own closed-form / analytic identity — catches transcription and coefficient errors, but is not an external oracle — Two internal oracles, no external dataset. ({ "1" }) Closed-form round trip: the minimum detectable bias is produced by inverting the non-central chi-square tail on the non-centrality (raim::pbias) and is verified by feeding the resulting non-centrality back through raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf at the monitor's own threshold, which must return P_md. ({ "2" }) Injection vs analysis: a bias is written into the RF estimate of one axis and cross_raim::run_cross_raim is re-run, so the statistic compared against the analytic non-centrality is the one the monitor computed, not a re-derivation. A seeded Monte-Carlo of the statistic itself is carried in the tests as a third check; it is deliberately NOT in the report, because a sampled estimate would be slower and not reproducible bit-for-bit. ExternalDataset is declined: the quantity is the detection power of THIS monitor at THIS scenario's sigma allocation, and the sigma magnitudes the MDB is scaled by are Modelled representative inputs, not measurements. ReferenceImpl was also considered and declined — the Monte-Carlo samples the same statistic the analysis describes, so it catches transcription and coefficient errors but is not an independent implementation of the monitor. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (pre-registered 0135a9e4), blocked (stays MODELLED): SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2/brentq reproduce the report threshold (within { "5.4e-12" }), per-axis MDB, power curve, crossing multiple and ramp time-to-detect (within { "5.3e-10" }) on five configurations; the injected-fault statistics have no independent oracle and the sigma magnitudes are Modelled inputs, so the scoping awaits an owner decision

({ "1" }) Ida e volta de forma fechada: o viés mínimo detectável é produzido invertendo a cauda do qui-quadrado não central sobre a não centralidade (raim::pbias) e é verificado devolvendo a não centralidade resultante por raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf no limiar do próprio monitor, o que deve devolver P_md. ({ "2" }) Injeção contra análise: um viés é escrito na estimativa de RF de um eixo e cross_raim::run_cross_raim é executado de novo, de modo que a estatística comparada com a não centralidade analítica é a que o monitor calculou, não uma rededução. Um Monte Carlo semeado da própria estatística é mantido nos testes como terceira verificação; ele deliberadamente NÃO está no relatório, porque uma estimativa amostrada seria mais lenta e não reproduzível bit a bit. ExternalDataset é recusado: a grandeza é o poder de detecção DESTE monitor na alocação de sigma DESTE cenário, e as magnitudes de sigma pelas quais o MDB é escalonado são entradas representativas modeladas, não medições. # checked against its own closed-form / analytic identity — catches transcription and coefficient errors, but is not an external oracle — Mixed, and separated rather than pooled. The PATTERN underneath is externally validated and keeps its own row (a scipy.special.j1 fixture to < { "0.05" } dB in tests/validate_p1_orbital_footprint.rs, plus here the published Airy half-power abscissa x = { "1.61634" } located by bisection rather than assumed). The GEOMETRY is checked against closed-form triangle trigonometry written as a different expression from the dot product under test. The CONTAINMENT of the real beam by the approximate cone is derived algebra (28.019/√η vs { "29.479" } degrees per λ/D), so the correction can only be non-positive for η ≤ { "0.9035" }. The measured disagreement at the documented working point — { "0" } links in beam under the real pattern against { "28" } under the approximation, −{ "2.33" } satellites per epoch, worst epoch { "3" }, on { "76" } evaluated links — is therefore a MODELLED finding about the approximation, pinned as a regression literal (the nearest row sits { "0.069" }° from either beam edge, four orders of magnitude above any last-digit disagreement), not an externally validated coverage number.
